# Spec revision 39: what changed in the records

Generated by `tools/record_changes.py`; do not edit. Each generated record as committed at
`9ebf327` (2026-10-25: the records of revision 36's regeneration, which revisions 37 and 38 left as they
were and stage 3's open batch kept, D31), against the record as committed now, after revision 39's one
regeneration (version 2 stage 3, plan step 10: groups C, F and A, T-130 and T-014, built at steps 6–9 on
2026-10-25–26). Revision 39 changed rules, so a changed verdict here is a **new result under a new rule**,
reported beside revision 38's and version 1's verdicts and never in their place (`M11.F`); a premise passing
is never a finding (`M11.5`). It is not a
defect. A row's verdict is its outcome (a criterion, or a criterion under a sweep setting) or whether the
criterion caught the mutant (a mutation row). An effect **moved** when its mean difference changed by more than
the half-width revision 38's record stated; moved effects are listed only for rows whose verdict held. "Differ at
all" counts every field of a row on both sides that is not exactly revision 38's (the verdict, the seed count, the
flags, each readout's mean difference, half-width and p), including a field on one side only; each is listed
under its record, and "No change" means none differs. A record's hash line must differ from revision 38's, or the
tool refuses: an unchanged hash means it was not regenerated. A record with no row on both sides is refused too.

| Record | Hash at revision 38 | Hash now | Rows compared | Verdicts changed | Effects moved | Differ at all | Only at revision 38 | Only now |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `docs/phase_c_ensemble_record.md` | `636c4c594c8d` | `042166ee2147` | 24 | 1 | 8 | 446 | 0 | 1 |
| `docs/phase_d_ensemble_record.md` | `3549d7c45f4b` | `042166ee2147` | 25 | 2 | 4 | 286 | 0 | 0 |

In the level-occupancy record, which has no verdicts, "Verdicts changed" counts the rows whose values changed.

**Not compared here:**

- `docs/phase_c_diagnostic_record.md`: it has no machine-readable block; `git diff 9ebf327 -- docs/phase_c_diagnostic_record.md` shows what changed.
- `docs/review/phase_c_trace_seed7.md, docs/review/phase_c_chart_seed7.html`: one seed's run, rendered; S1.7a adds lock-in release rows by design, so `git diff 9ebf327` on them shows that and any change of trajectory.
- `docs/review/phase_b_trace_seed7.md, docs/review/phase_b_trace_seed7_nadia.md`: Phase B's seed-7 traces, rendered at Phase B's close (`3132c69`) and not regenerated since, so they are revision 38's.
- `docs/phase_c_completion_report.md §11`: generated from the Phase C records compared here (`tools/c16_report.py`).
- `docs/spec_coverage.md`: generated from the tests and the ensemble and mutation records compared here.
- `docs/phase_b_mutation_record.md`: the record of Phase B's close at commit `3132c69`, frozen (plan D5) and not regenerated, so it is revision 38's apart from the status line stage 1 added; `tools/mutation_gate.py` refuses to rewrite it.
- `docs/version_2_sweep_record.md, docs/version_2_status.md`: the status page is generated from the records compared here (`tools/version_2_status.py`); the sweep record is the stage 2 sweep's, rendered from its own cache (`tools/version_2_sweep.py`), and is not rerun until stage 3's stage-end sweep (plan step 11, D17), so it is revision 38's.
- `docs/review/group_g_readout.md`: revision 36's before-and-after measurement on 20 seeds (`tools/group_g_readout.py`): its "before" section is the engine before revision 36, kept as measured, and its "after" section is regenerated on each later engine, so `git diff 9ebf327` on it shows what revision 39 moved.
- `docs/review/stage_3_projection_trace.md, docs/review/stage_3_iposition_probe.md, docs/review/stage_3_t130_event_sizes.md, docs/review/stage_3_displaced_coping_probe.md`: stage 3's measurements before any rule changed (step 2's trace and probe and step 3's displaced coping probe, `tools/stage_3_projection_trace.py`, `tools/stage_3_iposition_probe.py` and `tools/stage_3_displaced_coping_probe.py`, in no record's hash) and step 1's T-130 comparison, new in stage 3; nothing at revision 38 to compare.
- `docs/review/stage_3_unit_mutation_gate.md, docs/review/stage_3_step_7_criteria.md, docs/review/stage_3_step_7_c2_mutants.md, docs/review/stage_3_step_8_criteria.md, docs/review/stage_3_step_9_criteria.md`: stage 3's measurements between the open batch's commits (D31): the unit mutation proofs (`tools/mutation_gate.py`) and steps 7's to 9's targeted criteria and mutant runs (`tools/stage_3_targeted_run.py`), new in stage 3; nothing at revision 38 to compare.
- `docs/version_1_status.md`: version 1's status page, frozen at the tag: `tests/test_version_1_status.py` checks that it is the tag's, byte for byte.
- `docs/review/phase_c_famdiag_seed7.json`: the seed-7 run as a family diagram (`tools/famdiag_export.py`); regenerated at each of stage 3's steps and at step 10 (`tools/famdiag_export.py --seed 7`), so `git diff 9ebf327` on it shows the change of trajectory under revision 39's rules.
